+// D113 (specs/sprint-alfred-epic/plans/po-decisions-2026-10-18.md): an asynchronous native producer is journalled by a
+// source-owned BEGIN ticket (the synchronous prefix of the effect, bound and inventoried under the registry lock) and closed
+// by a source-owned SETTLE step once the promise settles. The registry lock is never held across the await and is never left
+// behind. These cases are appended after the existing contract cases, so no earlier case id moves.
+test("D113 an asynchronous driver does not hold the registry lock across its await; the settled launch leaves the registry usable", async () => {
+  const f = fixture();
+  try {
+    activate(f);
+    const lockPath = join(f.root, "session-authority", "registry.lock");
+    const held = invoke(f, key("d113-async-launch"));
+    assert.equal(held.ok, true, held.code);
+    assert.equal(existsSync(lockPath), false, "premise: no registry lock before the launch");
+    let lockDuringAwait = "driver-not-awaited";
+    f.source.launchDriver = async () => {
+      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 15));
+      lockDuringAwait = existsSync(lockPath);
+      return { ok: true, value: { launched: true } };
+    };
+    const launched = await f.controller.launch({ invocation: held.value, packetBindingSha256: held.value.receipt.packetBindingSha256 });
+    assert.equal(launched.ok, true, launched.code);
+    assert.equal(lockDuringAwait, false, "the registry lock is released after the BEGIN ticket, not held across the await");
+    assert.equal(existsSync(lockPath), false, "the settled launch leaves no registry lock behind");
+    const next = invoke(f, key("d113-after-settle"));
+    assert.equal(next.ok, true, `a following prepare completes after the settle step: ${next.code}`);
+    assert.equal(existsSync(lockPath), false);
+  } finally { rmSync(f.repoDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
+});
+
+test("D113 a rejecting or declared-async effect is settled by the owner: the rejection surfaces and the registry lock is always released", async () => {
+  const f = fixture();

+    activate(f);

+    const resolved = await f.authorityLifecycle.run({ kind: "prepare" }, async () => { await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 5)); return 7; });
+    assert.equal(resolved, 7, "a declared async function is a promise-returning effect like any other");

+    await assert.rejects(() => f.authorityLifecycle.run({ kind: "prepare" }, () => Promise.reject(new Error("d113-driver-rejected"))),

+  } finally { rmSync(f.repoDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }

+  return new Set(ids).size === ids.length && new Set(ranks).size === ranks.length
+    && new Set(declared).size === declared.length;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Validate a host-supplied family catalogue: the `records` of a normalized `pipeline.model-family-discovery.v1`
+ * (the repo's existing family declaration), with the same exact shapes and invariants. It proves membership and
+ * order only; it grants nothing. Returns null when the shape is not exactly that.
+ */
+function normalizeFamilyCatalogue(catalogue) {
+  if (!Array.isArray(catalogue) || catalogue.length === 0 || catalogue.length > 2048) return null;
+  const releaseIds = new Set();
+  const modelIds = new Set();
+  const versionsByFamily = new Map();
+  let variantCount = 0;
+  for (const record of catalogue) {
+    if (!hasKeys(record, RELEASE_KEYS) || !id(record.releaseId) || releaseIds.has(record.releaseId)
+      || !validFamilyId(record.familyId) || !validVersion(record.version) || typeof record.released !== "boolean"
+      || !SHA.test(record.groupingEvidenceSha256 ?? "")
+      || !Array.isArray(record.variants) || record.variants.length < 1 || record.variants.length > 256) return null;
+    releaseIds.add(record.releaseId);
+    const seen = versionsByFamily.get(record.familyId) ?? new Set();
+    if (seen.has(versionKey(record.version))) return null; // two releases of one family at one version: ambiguous order
+    seen.add(versionKey(record.version));
+    versionsByFamily.set(record.familyId, seen);
+    for (const variant of record.variants) {
+      variantCount += 1;
+      if (variantCount > 8192 || !hasKeys(variant, VARIANT_KEYS) || !id(variant.modelId) || modelIds.has(variant.modelId)
+        || !(variant.canonicalModelId === null || id(variant.canonicalModelId))
+        || !(variant.aliasEvidenceSha256 === null || SHA.test(variant.aliasEvidenceSha256 ?? ""))
+        || (variant.canonicalModelId === null) !== (variant.aliasEvidenceSha256 === null)
+        || typeof variant.visible !== "boolean" || typeof variant.selectable !== "boolean"
+        || (variant.selectable && (!variant.visible || !record.released))
+        || !(variant.efforts === null || (Array.isArray(variant.efforts) && variant.efforts.length <= 128
+          && variant.efforts.every((effort) => typeof effort === "string" && CATALOGUE_EFFORT.test(effort))
+          && new Set(variant.efforts).size === variant.efforts.length))
+        || !(variant.capabilityEvidenceSha256 === null || SHA.test(variant.capabilityEvidenceSha256 ?? ""))
+        || (variant.efforts !== null && variant.capabilityEvidenceSha256 === null)
+        || variant.compatibilityBySlotSha256 === null || typeof variant.compatibilityBySlotSha256 !== "object"
+        || Array.isArray(variant.compatibilityBySlotSha256)
+        || Object.keys(variant.compatibilityBySlotSha256).length > 512
+        || Object.entries(variant.compatibilityBySlotSha256).some(([key, value]) => key.length > 512
+          || !(value === null || SHA.test(value ?? "")))) return null;
+      modelIds.add(variant.modelId);
+    }
+  }
+  return catalogue;
+}
+
+/**
+ * D99: a newer VERSION inside the family the PO approved is followed without a new confirmation. Membership and
+ * order come only from the declared family (signed policy entry) and the host catalogue; an id that is not a
+ * released, visible, selectable, offered, effort-capable member of that family is never followed. A different
+ * family is never touched. Returns the followed variant (or null) and the ids the family rule has accounted for.
+ */
+function followApprovedFamily({ selected, effort, available, approvedIds, records }) {
+  let anchor = null;
+  for (const record of records) {
+    const variant = record.variants.find((item) => item.modelId === selected.modelId);
+    if (variant) anchor = record;
+  }
+  if (anchor === null) return { ok: true, follow: null, accounted: new Set() };
+  if (anchor.familyId !== selected.familyId || compareModelFamilyVersions(anchor.version, selected.version) !== 0) {
+    return { ok: false, code: "MODEL-ROLE-FAMILY-DECLARATION-MISMATCH" };
+  }
+  const members = records.filter((record) => record.familyId === selected.familyId && record.released);
+  const admissible = (record) => record.variants.filter((variant) => variant.visible && variant.selectable);
+  const newer = members.filter((record) => compareModelFamilyVersions(record.version, selected.version) > 0);
+  const followable = newer.map((record) => ({ record, variants: admissible(record).filter((variant) =>
+    available.has(variant.modelId) && !approvedIds.has(variant.modelId)
+    && Array.isArray(variant.efforts) && variant.efforts.includes(effort)) })).filter((item) => item.variants.length > 0);
+  let follow = null;
+  if (followable.length > 0) {
+    const best = followable.reduce((top, item) => compareModelFamilyVersions(item.record.version, top.record.version) > 0 ? item : top);
+    if (best.variants.length === 1) follow = { modelId: best.variants[0].modelId, version: [...best.record.version] };
+  }
+  const reached = follow === null ? selected.version : follow.version;
+  const accounted = new Set(members.filter((record) => compareModelFamilyVersions(record.version, reached) <= 0)
+    .flatMap((record) => admissible(record).map((variant) => variant.modelId)));
+  return { ok: true, follow, accounted };
 }

+/**
+ * D99: under one UNCHANGED signed policy (same policySha256), a strictly newer version of the SAME declared family
+ * is not a change the human has to confirm. A different family, an equal or lower version, or a receipt that does

+function familyUpgrade(before, after) {

+// SPDX-License-Identifier: SUL-1.0
+/**
+ * Wire shape of the onboarding driver result printed by scripts/onboarding-init.mjs
+ * (pipeline.onboarding-init.v1). The library result (`driveOnboardingInit`) keeps every
+ * published action in each place a caller may read it; the printed form states each one once:
+ *
+ * - `pending-asks` and `collect-input` outcomes: the top-level `pendingAsks` is the single
+ *   copy; `final.nextAction.pendingAsks` and `collectInput.pendingAsks` are dropped.
+ * - `collect-input` outcome: `collectInput` is the single full copy of the primary action;
+ *   `final.nextAction` becomes `{ kind, see: "collectInput" }`.
+ *
+ * A copy is dropped only when it is canonically equal to the copy that is kept, so a divergent
+ * copy is never hidden or replaced. `resolvePrintedOnboardingInit()` is the inverse for a
+ * consumer that needs the library shape: it resolves the reference and restores the asks.
+ */
+
+function canonical(value) {
+  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
+  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object") {
+    return `{${Object.keys(value).sort().map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonical(value[key])}`).join(",")}}`;
+  }
+  return JSON.stringify(value);
+}
+
+const PRINTED_ONCE_OUTCOMES = ["pending-asks", "collect-input"];
+const withoutAsks = (action) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(action).filter(([key]) => key !== "pendingAsks"));
+
+export function printOnboardingInitResult(result) {
+  if (!PRINTED_ONCE_OUTCOMES.includes(result?.outcome)) return result;
+  const asks = result.pendingAsks;
+  const dropAsks = (action) => (Array.isArray(asks) && asks.length > 0 && Array.isArray(action?.pendingAsks)
+    && canonical(action.pendingAsks) === canonical(asks) ? withoutAsks(action) : action);
+  let nextAction = result.final?.nextAction ? dropAsks(result.final.nextAction) : undefined;
+  const collectInput = result.collectInput ? dropAsks(result.collectInput) : undefined;
+  if (result.outcome === "collect-input" && nextAction && collectInput && canonical(nextAction) === canonical(collectInput)) {
+    nextAction = { kind: collectInput.kind, see: "collectInput" };
+  }
+  return { ...result,
+    ...(collectInput ? { collectInput } : {}),
+    ...(nextAction ? { final: { ...result.final, nextAction } } : {}) };
+}
+
+export function resolvePrintedOnboardingInit(printed) {
+  if (!PRINTED_ONCE_OUTCOMES.includes(printed?.outcome)) return printed;
+  const asks = Array.isArray(printed.pendingAsks) && printed.pendingAsks.length > 0 ? printed.pendingAsks : null;
+  const withAsks = (action) => (action && asks && action.pendingAsks === undefined ? { ...action, pendingAsks: asks } : action);
+  const collectInput = withAsks(printed.collectInput);
+  const reference = printed.final?.nextAction?.see === "collectInput" && collectInput;
+  const nextAction = reference ? collectInput : withAsks(printed.final?.nextAction);
+  if (collectInput === printed.collectInput && nextAction === printed.final?.nextAction) return printed;
+  return { ...printed,
+    ...(collectInput ? { collectInput } : {}),
+    ...(nextAction ? { final: { ...printed.final, nextAction } } : {}) };
+}

+const fold = (values) => Object.freeze(values.map((value) => value.toLowerCase()));
+const HOME_ENTRIES_FOLDED = fold(PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.homeRelativePaths);
+const PROFILE_ENTRIES_FOLDED = fold(PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.windowsProfileRelativePaths);
+const WINDOWS_SYSTEM_FOLDED = fold(PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.windowsSystemRoots);
+const NATIVE_DRIVE = /^[a-z]:(?:[\\/](.*))?$/iu;
+const WSL_DRIVE = /^\/mnt(?:\/([a-z])(?:\/(.*))?)?$/u;
+const WINDOWS_PROFILE = /^users\/([^/]+)(?:\/(.*))?$/iu;
+const OTHER_USER_HOME = /^\/(?:home|Users)\/([^/]+)(?:\/(.*))?$/u;
+
+function classifyHostPath(identity, homePrefixes) {
+  let rest = null;
+  const native = NATIVE_DRIVE.exec(identity);
+  if (native !== null) rest = (native[1] ?? "").replaceAll("\\", "/");
+  else {
+    const wsl = WSL_DRIVE.exec(identity);
+    if (wsl !== null) rest = wsl[1] === undefined ? "" : (wsl[2] ?? "");
+  }
+  if (rest !== null) {
+    const profile = WINDOWS_PROFILE.exec(rest);
+    return profile === null ? { windows: true, drive: true, profile: false, rest }
+      : { windows: true, drive: true, profile: true, rest: profile[2] ?? "" };
+  }
+  for (const prefix of homePrefixes) {
+    if (identity === prefix) return { windows: false, drive: false, profile: false, rest: "" };
+    if (identity.startsWith(`${prefix}/`)) return { windows: false, drive: false, profile: false, rest: identity.slice(prefix.length + 1) };
+  }
+  const other = OTHER_USER_HOME.exec(identity);
+  return other === null ? null : { windows: false, drive: false, profile: false, rest: other[2] ?? "" };
+}
+
+function homeRestRefused(rest, windows, recursive, directoryListing) {
+  const probe = windows ? rest.toLowerCase() : rest;
+  if (probe === "") return recursive || directoryListing;
+  const entries = windows ? [...HOME_ENTRIES_FOLDED, ...PROFILE_ENTRIES_FOLDED] : PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.homeRelativePaths;
+  return entries.some((entry) => probe === entry || probe.startsWith(`${entry}/`)
+    || (recursive && entry.startsWith(`${probe}/`)));
+}
+
+function hostCredentialRefusal(info, recursive, directoryListing) {
+  if (info.drive && !info.profile) {
+    if (info.rest === "") return recursive;
+    const segments = info.rest.toLowerCase().split("/");
+    if (WINDOWS_SYSTEM_FOLDED.includes(segments[0])) return true;
+    return segments[0] === "users" && segments.length === 1 && recursive;
+  }
+  return homeRestRefused(info.rest, info.windows, recursive, directoryListing);
+}
+
+function hostException(info, now) {
+  if (info === null || (info.drive && !info.profile)) return null;
+  const rest = info.windows ? info.rest.toLowerCase() : info.rest;
+  return PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.namedExceptions.find((entry) => {
+    if ((entry.windowsOnly && !info.windows) || Date.parse(`${entry.expires}T23:59:59.999Z`) < now) return false;
+    const path = info.windows ? entry.path.toLowerCase() : entry.path;
+    if (entry.kind === "file") return rest === path;
+    if (entry.kind === "dir") return rest === path || rest.startsWith(`${path}/`);
+    if (entry.kind === "files-with-suffix") {
+      const suffix = info.windows ? entry.suffix.toLowerCase() : entry.suffix;
+      const name = rest.slice(path.length + 1);
+      return rest.startsWith(`${path}/`) && !name.includes("/") && name.endsWith(suffix) && name.length > suffix.length;
+    }
+    return false;
+  }) ?? null;
+}
+
+/** True when `identity` (an absolute path, either spelling) lies in a name-based credential location and no live named exception covers it. */
+export function isHostCredentialLocation(identity, { homePrefixes = [], recursive = false, directoryListing = false, now = Date.now() } = {}) {
+  if (typeof identity !== "string" || identity === "") return false;
+  const info = classifyHostPath(identity, homePrefixes);
+  return info !== null && hostException(info, now) === null && hostCredentialRefusal(info, recursive, directoryListing);
+}
+
+// PO decision D96 (2026-10-22, with the 2026-09-10 correction in docs/adr/draft-read-scope-containment-boundary.md):
+// a passive read outside the repository is not by itself a denial reason. Every absolute host path qualifies here;
+// the credential roots, system pseudo roots and secret basenames are screened for BOTH identities by the caller
+// before this predicate is reached.
+function userVisibleHostPath(path) {
   if (process.platform === "win32") return /^[a-z]:[\\/]/iu.test(path) && !systemPseudoPath(path);
-  return [homeDir, "/home", "/mnt", "/media", "/tmp", "/private/tmp",
-    "/Users", "/Volumes", "/workspace", "/workspaces", "/var/folders", "/private/var/folders"]
-    .some((root) => within(root, path));
+  return isAbsolute(path);
 }

   return false;

+// Host-wide runner session and skill collections: a recursive directory search outside the repository stays
+// refused when it lies in, or contains, one of these (D96 admits outside directories, but not other sessions'
+// content). This is NOT a credential list; it only bounds directory recursion and never widens a refusal elsewhere.
+const SESSION_COLLECTION_HOME_PATHS = Object.freeze([".codex/sessions", ".codex/skills", ".claude/projects"]);
+
+function touchesSessionCollection(homeDir, identities, realpath) {
+  return SESSION_COLLECTION_HOME_PATHS.some((relativeRoot) => {
+    const lexicalRoot = resolve(homeDir, relativeRoot);
+    let physicalRoot = lexicalRoot;
+    try { physicalRoot = realpath(lexicalRoot); }
+    catch (error) { if (error?.code !== "ENOENT") return true; }
+    return identities.some((identity) => [lexicalRoot, physicalRoot].some((root) =>
+      within(root, identity) || within(identity, root)));
+  });
+}

+test("D109 other users' home directories get the same protections as the current user's", (t) => {
+  if (process.platform === "win32") { t.skip("POSIX host paths"); return; }
+  const { admits, admitsDir } = d109();
+  for (const entry of [".kube/config", ".config/gcloud/properties", ".aws/cli/cache/x", ".docker/config.json", ".azure/accessTokens.json",

+  }

+  assert.equal(admits("/home/bob/projects/a.md"), true);
+  assert.equal(admits("/home/bob/.config/git/config"), true);
+  // The current user's linked home counts by its physical name as well.
+  const linked = d109("/home/alice", { links: { "/home/alice": "/data/alice-home", "/home/alice/.kube/config": "/data/alice-home/.kube/config" } });
+  assert.equal(linked.admits("/home/alice/.kube/config"), false);
+});

+test("D109 named exceptions: exact files and the public-key suffix are admitted; their siblings and symlink tricks stay refused; expiry ends them", (t) => {
+  if (process.platform === "win32") { t.skip("POSIX host paths"); return; }
+  const table = policyModule.PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.namedExceptions;
+  assert.ok(Array.isArray(table) && Object.isFrozen(table) && table.length >= 5, "the exceptions are a frozen table in the declaration");
+  for (const entry of table) {
+    for (const field of ["id", "path", "kind", "reason", "owner", "decision", "expires"]) assert.equal(typeof entry[field], "string", `${entry.id} lacks ${field}`);
+    assert.match(entry.expires, /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/u);
+    assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(entry));
+  }
+  const { admits } = d109();
+  for (const path of ["/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts", "~/.ssh/known_hosts", "/home/alice/.ssh/config", "/home/alice/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub",
+    "/home/alice/.aws/config", "/home/bob/.ssh/known_hosts", "/home/bob/.ssh/id_rsa.pub"]) {
+    assert.equal(admits(path), true, `${path} is a named exception`);
+  }
+  for (const path of ["/home/alice/.ssh/id_ed25519", "/home/alice/.ssh/authorized_keys", "/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts.old", "/home/alice/.ssh/sub/known_hosts",
+    "/home/alice/.aws/credentials", "/home/alice/.aws/config.d/x", "/home/alice/.ssh/.pub", "/home/alice/.ssh/nested/key.pub"]) {
+    assert.equal(admits(path), false, `${path} must stay refused`);
+  }
+  const trick = d109("/home/alice", { links: { "/home/alice/.ssh/alias.pub": "/home/alice/.ssh/id_rsa", "/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts": "/home/alice/.ssh/id_rsa" } });
+  assert.equal(trick.admits("/home/alice/.ssh/alias.pub"), false, "a .pub name that resolves to a private key");
+  assert.equal(trick.admits("/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts"), false, "a known_hosts link to a private key");
+  const expired = Date.parse("2099-01-01T00:00:00Z");
+  assert.equal(admits("/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts", { now: expired }), false, "an expired exception is ignored");
+});

+test("D109 WSL view of the Windows drive: AppData is refused as a whole, with Local/Temp as the named exception; system and profile roots too", (t) => {
+  if (process.platform === "win32") { t.skip("WSL paths are POSIX spellings"); return; }
+  const { admits, admitsDir } = d109();
+  const refused = [];
+  for (const drive of ["c", "d"]) {
+    for (const user of ["Bob", "alice"]) {
+      refused.push(`/mnt/${drive}/Users/${user}/AppData/Roaming/Microsoft/Protect/S-1-5-21/x`, `/mnt/${drive}/Users/${user}/AppData/Local/Google/Chrome/User Data/Default/Login Data`,
+        `/mnt/${drive}/Users/${user}/AppData/Local/Anything/state.txt`, `/mnt/${drive}/Users/${user}/AppData/Roaming/npm/x.txt`,
+        `/mnt/${drive}/Users/${user}/AppData/Local/Microsoft/Credentials/x`, `/mnt/${drive}/Users/${user}/AppData/Roaming/Mozilla/Firefox/Profiles/x/key4.db`,

+    }
+  }

+  for (const path of refused) assert.equal(admits(path), false, `${path} must be refused`);
+  for (const path of ["/mnt/c/Users/Bob/AppData/Local/Temp/build.log", "/mnt/d/Users/alice/AppData/Local/Temp/sub/x.txt", "/mnt/c/Users/Bob/Documents/report.md",

+    assert.equal(admits(path), true, `${path} must stay admitted`);
+  }
+  for (const path of ["/mnt", "/mnt/c", "/mnt/c/Users", "/mnt/c/Users/Bob", "/mnt/c/Users/Bob/AppData", "/mnt/c/Windows"]) {
+    assert.equal(admitsDir(path, { recursive: true }), false, `recursive ${path} must be refused`);
+  }
+  assert.equal(admitsDir("/mnt/c/Users/Bob", { directoryListing: true }), false, "listing a profile root exposes credential roots, as for the home directory");
+  assert.equal(admitsDir("/mnt/c", { directoryListing: true }), true);
+  assert.equal(admitsDir("/mnt/c/Users", { directoryListing: true }), true);
+  assert.equal(admitsDir("/mnt/c/Users/Bob/Documents", { directoryListing: true }), true);
+  const expired = Date.parse("2099-01-01T00:00:00Z");
+  assert.equal(admits("/mnt/c/Users/Bob/AppData/Local/Temp/build.log", { now: expired }), false, "the Temp exception expires");
+  const link = d109("/home/alice", { links: { "/opt/shortcut/x.txt": "/mnt/c/Users/Bob/AppData/Roaming/x.txt" } });
+  assert.equal(link.admits("/opt/shortcut/x.txt"), false, "a link into AppData is judged by its physical name");
+});

+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// FAIL-CLOSED CONDITIONS (PO decision D86, 2026-10-22: "EL-01 tripwire fails open ... fixed").
+// These cases drive guard-el01-tripwire.mjs directly. The rule they pin: in a governed
+// repository, when the tripwire CANNOT EVALUATE the evidence it enforces on (a corrupt or

+  });

+function assertRefusedWithRepair(res, causeNeedle) {

+// Report-bound phase-two adjudication (ALF-K33). A phase-two Reader may expressly decline a phase-one finding; the
+// closed form records that as `adjudicated`, bound to the round's OWN immutable phase-two report by path, digest and a
+// literal quote from each report. It is neither a correction nor an owner acceptance, and it infers neither.
+const adjudicated = (fx, index, over = {}) => {
+  const ref = fx.record.rounds[index].phaseTwo;
+  return { id: "F2", class: "reordering", status: "adjudicated", resolutionCommit: null,
+    adjudication: { report: { path: ref.path, sha256: ref.sha256 }, phaseOneQuote: "# Synthetic phase-one", phaseTwoQuote: "# Synthetic phase-two", ...over } };
+};

+test("a phase-two no-change adjudication closes a finding without a resolution commit or owner acceptance (RED: unchanged checker has no such status)", (t) => {
+  const fx = shared(t);
+  const [ok] = fx.trial(() => fx.editDisposition(0, (d) => { d.findings.push(adjudicated(fx, 0)); }));
+  assert.equal(ok.status, "passed", joined(ok));
+  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(fx.read(fx.ownerPath)).acceptedFindingIds, []); // nothing was accepted on anyone's behalf
+  const [onlyAdjudicated] = fx.trial(() => fx.editDisposition(3, (d) => { d.findings = [adjudicated(fx, 3)]; }));
+  assert.equal(onlyAdjudicated.status, "passed", joined(onlyAdjudicated)); // the round's correction batch still exists and is checked
+  const [ownerKey] = fx.trial(() => {
+    fx.editDisposition(0, (d) => { d.findings.push(adjudicated(fx, 0)); });
+    fx.editJson(fx.ownerPath, (o) => { o.acceptedFindingIds = ["round-1/F2"]; });
+    fx.editRecord((r) => { r.ownerDecision.sha256 = hash(fx.read(fx.ownerPath)); });
+  });
+  assert.equal(ownerKey.status, "failed", joined(ownerKey)); // an adjudication is not an acceptance and may not be listed as one
+});

+  const unreused = await mirror("adj-reuse", [["||quotes.has(a.phaseOneQuote)", ""]]);
+  const [reused, redReused] = fx.trial(() => fx.editDisposition(0, (d) => { d.findings.push(adjudicated(fx, 0), { ...adjudicated(fx, 0), id: "F3" }); }), [checkReaderBinding, unreused]);
+  assert.equal(reused.status, "failed", joined(reused)); assert.match(joined(reused), /adjudication quote reused/);
+  assert.equal(redReused.status, "passed");
+  const [same] = fx.trial(() => fx.editDisposition(0, (d) => { d.findings.push(adjudicated(fx, 0, { phaseTwoQuote: "# Synthetic phase-one" })); }));
+  assert.equal(same.status, "failed", joined(same)); // one sentence cannot stand for both reports; it would not be in the phase-two report either
+});

+    if (status === "open") assert.equal(red.status, "passed");
+  }

+// --- AC-135 (ALF-K32): the admitted CLI path reaches the answered-receipt verifier ---
+// The host-owned executor is a sealed host dependency (never stdin input). The
+// stand-in for Codex is a closed, managed Node process that computes its answer
+// from the question it receives; no Codex CLI, model call or export runs.
+const MANAGED_FIXTURE = `"use strict";
+const { createHash } = require("node:crypto");
+let text = "";
+process.stdin.setEncoding("utf8");
+process.stdin.on("data", (chunk) => { text += chunk; });
+process.stdin.on("end", () => {
+  const { question, model, effort, transport } = JSON.parse(text);
+  const answer = "Managed fixture answer " + createHash("sha256").update(question).digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
+  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({
+    status: "answered", answer, fixturePid: process.pid, identity: { provider: "openai", modelId: model, effort },
+    sandboxExecution: {
+      schema: "pipeline.codex-sandbox-host-execution.v1", selectionId: transport.selectionId, selectionSha256: transport.selectionSha256,
+      repoFingerprint: transport.repoFingerprint, duty: "advisory", dispatch: transport.dispatch,
+      observed: { cliSha256: transport.cliSha256, profileSha256: transport.profileSha256, networkEnabled: true, scratchRootSha256: transport.scratchRootSha256 },
+      terminal: { childStarted: true, exitCode: 0, stdioStatus: "complete", cleanupStatus: "complete" },
+    },
+  }));
+});
+`;
+function routeDependencies() {
+  return {
+    resolveAdvisoryRoute: ({ candidateCommit: commit }) => ({
+      dutyId: "advisory", runner: "codex", model: ANSWER_MODEL, effort: "max", state: "default", sourceSha256: "a".repeat(64), candidateCommit: commit,
+    }),
+    selectModelRoleForTaskFn: () => { throw new Error("optional store unavailable"); },
+  };
+}
+/** Executor built from the managed Node fixture; `launches` records each real process it started. */
+function managedExecutor(dir, launches, options = {}) {
+  const fixturePath = join(dir, "managed-codex-fixture.cjs");
+  writeFileSync(fixturePath, MANAGED_FIXTURE);
+  return async ({ input }) => runSelectedAdvisoryHost(input, answeredTransport(input, {
+    ...options,
+    invoke: (sandboxTransport) => {
+      const run = spawnSync(process.execPath, [fixturePath], {
+        encoding: "utf8",
+        input: JSON.stringify({
+          question: input.question, model: ANSWER_MODEL, effort: "max",
+          transport: {
+            selectionId: sandboxTransport.selectionId, selectionSha256: sandboxTransport.selectionSha256, repoFingerprint: sandboxTransport.repoFingerprint,
+            dispatch: sandboxTransport.dispatch, cliSha256: sandboxTransport.toolchain.cliSha256, profileSha256: sandboxTransport.profile.sha256,
+            scratchRootSha256: sandboxTransport.profile.scratchRootSha256,
+          },
+        }),
+      });
+      assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
+      const result = JSON.parse(run.stdout);
+      launches.push(result.fixturePid);
+      return result;
+    },
+  }));
+}
+const admitted = async () => ({ status: "admitted" });
+function admittedDependencies(executeAdmittedConsult, extra = {}) {
+  return {
+    readRepositoryConsent: () => "approved", admitOrdinaryConsult: admitted, hostObservation: WSL_RELEASE, ...routeDependencies(), executeAdmittedConsult,
+    // The decision-event journal writes into the repository; it is stubbed here and exercised by its own suite.
+    recordAdvisoryDecisionEvent: async () => ({ appended: false, code: "AGENT_DECISION_EVENT_STUBBED" }),
+    ...extra,
+  };

+    ];
+    for (const [name, code, executor] of cases) {
+      const run = await runCliBridge(dir, name, admittedDependencies(executor));
+      assert.equal(run.code, 2, `${name}: non-zero exit`);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.ok, false, `${name}: no answer`);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.code, code, `${name}: typed code`);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.answer, null, `${name}: the unverified answer is never emitted`);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.repair, ANSWERED_RECEIPT_REFUSALS[code], `${name}: the repair is named`);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.sandboxBinding ?? null, null);
+      const receipt = JSON.parse(readFileSync(run.receiptPath, "utf8"));
+      assert.equal(validateAdvisoryReceipt(receipt).ok, true);
+      assert.equal(receipt.observed.status, "unavailable", `${name}: the receipt claims no answered execution`);
+      assert.equal(receipt.answerSha256, null);
+      assert.equal(receipt.observed.identity, null);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.routeSelectionPath ?? null, null, `${name}: a possibly-started attempt is not described as a no-child decision`);
+    }
+    // An executor that throws or returns nothing is a typed unavailable outcome, not an answer.
+    for (const [name, executor] of [["throws", async () => { throw new Error("host executor failed"); }], ["nothing", async () => undefined]]) {
+      const run = await runCliBridge(dir, name, admittedDependencies(executor));
+      assert.equal(run.code, 2);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.ok, false);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.code, "advisor-host-execution-unavailable");
+      assert.equal(run.completed.answer, null);
+      assert.equal(typeof run.completed.repair, "string");
+      assert.equal(JSON.stringify(run.events).includes("host executor failed"), false, "no raw error text is reported");
+    }
+  } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
+});
+
+test("AC-135: every no-child refusal names a repair or alternative the user can act on", { skip: HOST_LIMITATION }, async () => {
+  const dir = scratchDir("wsl-pin-repair");
+  try {
+    const cases = [
+      ["callback", { readRepositoryConsent: () => "approved" }, "ordinary-consult-host-callback-unavailable"],
+      ["route", { readRepositoryConsent: () => "approved", admitOrdinaryConsult: async () => ({ status: "unavailable" }) }, "ordinary-consult-host-route-unavailable"],
+      ["denied", { readRepositoryConsent: () => "approved", admitOrdinaryConsult: async () => ({ status: "denied" }) }, "advisor-host-export-denied"],
+      ["declined", { readRepositoryConsent: () => "declined" }, "advisor-repository-export-declined"],
+    ];
+    const seen = new Set();
+    for (const [name, deps, code] of cases) {
+      const run = await runCliBridge(dir, name, deps);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.code, code);
+      assert.equal(typeof run.completed.repair, "string", `${name}: a repair is named`);
+      assert.equal(run.completed.repair.length > 60, true, `${name}: actionable text`);
+      assert.match(run.completed.repair, /no model was called/iu, `${name}: states that nothing ran`);
+      assert.match(run.completed.repair, /continue without an advisor/iu, `${name}: names the alternative`);
+      seen.add(run.completed.repair);
+    }
+    assert.equal(seen.size, 4, "each outcome has its own repair");
+    const denied = await runCliBridge(dir, "denied-repair", { readRepositoryConsent: () => "approved", admitOrdinaryConsult: async () => ({ status: "denied" }) });
+    assert.match(denied.completed.repair, /do not retry/iu, "a denied export is not retried through another command or provider");
+  } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
+});

+// --- AC-135 (ALF-K32, PO tile 2026-10-22): WSL is admitted only behind a passing sandbox preflight ---
+// The WSL deferral stays the default; the existing preflight receipt (codex-sandbox-preflight.mjs) is the
+// only evidence that lifts it. These are fixtures: no Codex CLI, sandbox or model call runs here.
+import * as routeNs from "../../scripts/codex-host-advisor-route.mjs";
+import { validatePreflightReceipt } from "../../scripts/codex-sandbox-preflight.mjs";
+function wslPreflightReceipt(patch = {}) {
+  const receipt = {
+    schema: "pipeline.codex-sandbox-preflight.v1", cli: { version: "0.144.6", artifactSha256: "a".repeat(64) },
+    sandboxTransport: { selection: "codex-cli-owned" }, observedHelper: { role: "diagnostic-only", artifactSha256: null },
+    platform: { os: "linux", kernelClass: "wsl2", filesystemClass: "wsl-native" },
+    profile: { id: "codex-critic-intermediate.v1", rawSha256: "b".repeat(64), compiledStateSha256: "c".repeat(64) }, networkEnabled: true,
+    vectors: { allowedRead: true, externalReadDenied: false, sensitiveReadDenied: false, writeDenied: true, scratchWriteAllowed: true, networkDenied: false,
+      childStdioEquivalent: true, stdinEofEquivalent: true, childExitEquivalent: true, appServerInitEquivalent: true, lifecycleComplete: true },
+    appServer: { control: { appServerInitialized: true, appServerBoundedStop: true, appServerErrorClass: null }, sandbox: { appServerInitialized: true, appServerBoundedStop: true, appServerErrorClass: null } },
+    canaries: { count: 4, manifestSha256: "d".repeat(64), unchanged: true }, eventChainSha256: "e".repeat(64), durationMs: 1200,
+    eligibility: "intermediate", terminalCode: "ok", ...patch,
+  };
+  return receipt;
+}
+const FEATURE_APPROVED = { runner: "codex", profile: "feature", consent: "approved" };
+
+test("AC-135: the fixture receipt is a valid existing preflight receipt", () => {
+  assert.doesNotThrow(() => validatePreflightReceipt(wslPreflightReceipt()));
+});
+
+test("AC-135: WSL with a passing intermediate WSL preflight receipt resolves to the host route; anything else stays deferred with one runnable repair", () => {
+  for (const observation of [WSL_RELEASE, WSL_DISTRO]) {
+    assert.equal(routeNs.selectHostAdvisorRouteForHost(FEATURE_APPROVED, observation, wslPreflightReceipt()), ROUTES.HOST);
+    const resolved = routeNs.resolveHostAdvisorRouteForHost(FEATURE_APPROVED, observation, wslPreflightReceipt());
+    assert.equal(resolved.route, ROUTES.HOST);
+    assert.equal(resolved.policy.maxAttempts, 1);
+    const failing = [
+      undefined, null, {}, "ok",
+      wslPreflightReceipt({ eligibility: "none", terminalCode: "canary-drift" }),
+      wslPreflightReceipt({ terminalCode: "permission-mismatch" }),
+      wslPreflightReceipt({ eligibility: "diagnostic" }),
+      wslPreflightReceipt({ platform: { os: "linux", kernelClass: "linux", filesystemClass: "native-linux" } }),
+      wslPreflightReceipt({ canaries: { count: 4, manifestSha256: "d".repeat(64), unchanged: false } }),
+      { ...wslPreflightReceipt(), extra: true },
+    ];
+    for (const receipt of failing) {
+      assert.equal(routeNs.selectHostAdvisorRouteForHost(FEATURE_APPROVED, observation, receipt), ROUTES.WSL_UNAVAILABLE, `deferred for ${JSON.stringify(receipt)?.slice(0, 40)}`);
+      const denied = routeNs.resolveHostAdvisorRouteForHost(FEATURE_APPROVED, observation, receipt);
+      assert.equal(denied.route, ROUTES.WSL_UNAVAILABLE);
+      assert.equal(denied.policy, null);
+      assert.equal(denied.reason, "wsl-sandbox-preflight-not-passed");
+      assert.equal(denied.repair, routeNs.WSL_PREFLIGHT_REPAIR);
+    }
+  }
+  assert.match(routeNs.WSL_PREFLIGHT_REPAIR, /codex-sandbox-preflight\.mjs --run --kind intermediate --codex \S+ --receipt \S+/u);
+  assert.equal(routeNs.WSL_PREFLIGHT_REPAIR.includes("\n"), false, "one command");
+  // A passing receipt changes nothing off WSL and never lifts a decline or the mini profile.
+  assert.equal(routeNs.selectHostAdvisorRouteForHost(FEATURE_APPROVED, NON_WSL, wslPreflightReceipt()), ROUTES.HOST);
+  assert.equal(routeNs.selectHostAdvisorRouteForHost({ ...FEATURE_APPROVED, consent: "declined" }, WSL_RELEASE, wslPreflightReceipt()), ROUTES.NO_CONSENT);
+  assert.equal(routeNs.selectHostAdvisorRouteForHost({ ...FEATURE_APPROVED, profile: "mini" }, WSL_RELEASE, wslPreflightReceipt()), ROUTES.PROFILE);
+  // The 2-argument form (no preflight evidence) is unchanged.
+  assert.equal(selectHostAdvisorRouteForHost(FEATURE_APPROVED, WSL_RELEASE), ROUTES.WSL_UNAVAILABLE);
+});
+
+test("AC-135: the bridge's selected-sandbox entry honours a passing WSL preflight and otherwise returns the repair, with no launch", async () => {
+  const input = advisoryInput();
+  let launches = 0;
+  const failing = await runCodexAdvisoryThroughSelectedSandbox(input, async () => { launches += 1; }, {
+    hostObservation: WSL_RELEASE, repoRoot, sandboxPreflight: wslPreflightReceipt({ terminalCode: "canary-drift", eligibility: "none" }),
+    observeWorkspace: () => { throw new Error("must not observe"); },
+  });
+  assert.equal(failing.advisoryResult.code, "advisory_unavailable_wsl_native_deferred");
+  assert.equal(failing.advisoryResult.repair, routeNs.WSL_PREFLIGHT_REPAIR);
+  assert.equal(failing.advisoryResult.answer, null);
+  assert.equal(failing.advisoryResult.receipt, null);
+  assert.equal(launches, 0);
+  const passingInput = advisoryInput();
+  const passed = await runCodexAdvisoryThroughSelectedSandbox(passingInput, async () => { launches += 1; }, {
+    ...answeredTransport(passingInput), hostObservation: WSL_RELEASE, sandboxPreflight: wslPreflightReceipt(),
+    observeWorkspace: () => ({ workspaceSha256: "f".repeat(64) }),
+  });
+  assert.equal(passed.advisoryResult.ok, true, JSON.stringify(passed.advisoryResult));
+  assert.equal(passed.advisoryResult.code, "answered");
+  assert.equal(launches, 0, "the JSON adapter is still ignored");
+  assert.equal(verifyAnsweredAdvisoryReceipt(boundParts(passingInput, passed)).ok, true);
+});

+// Populated pendingAsks (the case the test above explicitly does not exercise). The onboarding
+// driver prints a published ask ONCE. Measured on a fresh repository with the codex runner
+// (outcome "collect-input", one ~3.1 KB bundled initial-answers ask), before the fix:

+const ONBOARDING_INIT_CLI = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../scripts/onboarding-init.mjs", import.meta.url));
+const COLLECT_INPUT_OUTPUT_BOUND = 19_000;
+
+function driverOutput(runner) {
+  const root = mkdtempTestScratch("alf-k15-root-");
+  const home = mkdtempTestScratch("alf-k15-home-");
+  const run = spawnSync(process.execPath, [ONBOARDING_INIT_CLI, "--root", root, "--runner", runner], {
+    encoding: "utf8", shell: false, maxBuffer: 8 * 1024 * 1024,
+    env: { ...process.env, PIPELINE_ONBOARDING_HOMEDIR_OVERRIDE: home },
+  });
+  rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
+  return { stdout: run.stdout, parsed: JSON.parse(run.stdout) };
+}
+
+function assertAsksPrintedOnce({ stdout, parsed }, label) {
+  assert.ok(Array.isArray(parsed.pendingAsks) && parsed.pendingAsks.length > 0, `${label}: fixture must carry real asks`);
+  for (const ask of parsed.pendingAsks) {
+    assert.equal(countOf(stdout, JSON.stringify(ask)), 1, `${label}: an ask is serialized more than once`);
+  }
+  assert.equal(parsed.final?.nextAction?.pendingAsks, undefined, `${label}: ask repeated inside final.nextAction`);
+  assert.equal(parsed.collectInput?.pendingAsks, undefined, `${label}: ask repeated inside collectInput`);
+}
+
+test("[pendingAsks compaction] the driver prints each published ask once for the collect-input outcome and stays within its size bound", () => {
+  const codex = driverOutput("codex");
+  assert.equal(codex.parsed.outcome, "collect-input", "fixture sanity: the codex first stop is a collect-input outcome with sibling asks");
+  assertAsksPrintedOnce(codex, "codex/collect-input");
+  // The primary question stays reachable: in full in collectInput, by kind in final.nextAction.
+  assert.equal(codex.parsed.collectInput.kind, "collect-input");
+  assert.equal(codex.parsed.final.nextAction.kind, "collect-input");
+  assert.ok(bytes(codex.stdout) <= COLLECT_INPUT_OUTPUT_BOUND, `driver output ${bytes(codex.stdout)} exceeds ${COLLECT_INPUT_OUTPUT_BOUND}`);
+  const claude = driverOutput("claude");
+  assert.equal(claude.parsed.outcome, "pending-asks");
+  assertAsksPrintedOnce(claude, "claude/pending-asks");
+});
+
+// PO decision (tile, 2026-10-22): the primary collect-input action is printed once too. On the wire
+// final.nextAction is a short reference to collectInput; resolvePrintedOnboardingInit() gives a
+// consumer that needs the library shape the full action back. Measured on the same fixture: after the
+// asks were printed once the output was 16,829 bytes with the primary action (~3 KB) printed twice.
+const COLLECT_INPUT_REFERENCE_BOUND = 15_000;
+
+test("[pendingAsks compaction] the collect-input primary action is printed once; final.nextAction is a resolvable reference", async () => {
+  const codex = driverOutput("codex");
+  const { parsed, stdout } = codex;
+  assert.equal(parsed.outcome, "collect-input");
+  assert.equal(countOf(stdout, JSON.stringify(parsed.collectInput)), 1, "the full primary action is printed more than once");
+  assert.deepEqual(parsed.final.nextAction, { kind: "collect-input", see: "collectInput" });
+  assert.ok(bytes(stdout) <= COLLECT_INPUT_REFERENCE_BOUND, `driver output ${bytes(stdout)} exceeds ${COLLECT_INPUT_REFERENCE_BOUND}`);
+  const { resolvePrintedOnboardingInit } = await import("../onboarding-driver-output.mjs");
+  const resolved = resolvePrintedOnboardingInit(parsed);
+  assert.deepEqual(resolved.final.nextAction, resolved.collectInput, "the reference resolves to the full action");
+  assert.deepEqual(resolved.collectInput.pendingAsks, parsed.pendingAsks, "the resolved action carries the library-shape asks");
+  assert.equal(resolvePrintedOnboardingInit(resolved).final.nextAction, resolved.final.nextAction, "resolving a resolved result is the identity");
+});
+
+test("printed shape round-trips to the library shape for collect-input and pending-asks, and ignores other outcomes", async () => {
+  const { printOnboardingInitResult, resolvePrintedOnboardingInit } = await import("../onboarding-driver-output.mjs");
+  const ask = { kind: "collect-input", inputs: [{ name: "a" }], applyAction: { argv: ["x"] } };
+  const primary = { kind: "collect-input", input: { name: "p" }, guidance: "g" };
+  const collect = { outcome: "collect-input", collectInput: { ...primary, pendingAsks: [ask] }, pendingAsks: [ask], final: { status: "s", nextAction: { ...primary, pendingAsks: [ask] } } };
+  const printed = printOnboardingInitResult(collect);
+  assert.equal(countOf(JSON.stringify(printed), JSON.stringify(ask)), 1);
+  assert.equal(countOf(JSON.stringify(printed), JSON.stringify(primary)), 1);
+  assert.deepEqual(resolvePrintedOnboardingInit(printed), collect);
+  const command = { kind: "command", executable: "node", argv: ["x"] };
+  const pending = { outcome: "pending-asks", pendingAsks: [ask], final: { nextAction: { ...command, pendingAsks: [ask] } } };
+  assert.deepEqual(printOnboardingInitResult(pending).final.nextAction, command, "the pending-asks outcome keeps its command, without the repeated asks");
+  assert.deepEqual(resolvePrintedOnboardingInit(printOnboardingInitResult(pending)), pending);
+  // A divergent copy is never hidden, a non-equal primary action is never replaced by a reference.
+  const divergent = { ...collect, final: { status: "s", nextAction: { ...primary, guidance: "other", pendingAsks: [{ ...ask, kind: "other" }] } } };
+  assert.deepEqual(printOnboardingInitResult(divergent).final.nextAction, divergent.final.nextAction);
+  const ready = { outcome: "ready", final: { nextAction: null } };
+  assert.equal(printOnboardingInitResult(ready), ready);
+  assert.equal(resolvePrintedOnboardingInit(ready), ready);
+});
+

+function capture(fn) {
+  const out = []; const err = [];
+  const beforeLog = console.log; const beforeError = console.error;
+  console.log = (...values) => out.push(values.join(" "));
+  console.error = (...values) => err.push(values.join(" "));
+  try { return { code: fn(), stdout: out.join("\n"), stderr: err.join("\n") }; }
+  finally { console.log = beforeLog; console.error = beforeError; }

+// ---- the sanitized review-chain digest pair ----
+const ORIGINAL = sha("original report, unsanitized");
+const PUBLISHED = sha("published report, sanitized");
+const pairReport = (name, published = PUBLISHED, original = ORIGINAL) => ({ path: name, sha256: published, originalSha256: original });

+test("D64 review chain: a sanitized report binds the original and the published digest and a delta after a formal correction needs no budget exception", () => {
+  const specV1 = FOUR_COLUMN;
+  const first = createDesignReviewReceipt({ featureId: "f1", previous: null, sources: sources(specV1), reviewer, report: pairReport("specs/f1/report-a.md"),
+    openFindingIds: ["F-FORMAT"], reviewedAt: "2026-10-10T00:00:00.000Z" });
+  assert.equal(first.ok, true, JSON.stringify(first));
+  assert.deepEqual(first.receipt.report, { path: "specs/f1/report-a.md", sha256: PUBLISHED, originalSha256: ORIGINAL });
+  assert.deepEqual(summarizeDesignReviewReceipt({ path: "specs/f1/receipt-1.json", bytes: first.bytes }).summary.report, first.receipt.report);
+  // The formal correction changes the Spec bytes only; the delta is round 2 of the same chain, so no exception is needed.
+  const corrected = createDesignReviewReceipt({ featureId: "f1", previous: { path: "specs/f1/receipt-1.json", bytes: first.bytes }, sources: sources(THREE_COLUMN),
+    reviewer, report: pairReport("specs/f1/report-b.md", sha("b-published"), sha("b-original")), openFindingIds: [], reviewedAt: "2026-10-11T00:00:00.000Z" });
+  assert.equal(corrected.ok, true, JSON.stringify(corrected));
+  assert.deepEqual([corrected.receipt.kind, corrected.receipt.round, corrected.receipt.verdict], ["delta", 2, "pass"]);
+  const binding = createDesignApprovalBinding({ featureId: "f1", receipts: [{ path: "specs/f1/receipt-1.json", bytes: first.bytes }, { path: "specs/f1/receipt-2.json", bytes: corrected.bytes }], currentSources: sources(THREE_COLUMN) });
+  assert.equal(binding.ok, true, JSON.stringify(binding));
+  assert.deepEqual(binding.binding.reviewReceipts.map((entry) => entry.round), [1, 2]);
+  // The pair is part of what the receipt digest binds: the same review with another original digest is another receipt.
+  const other = createDesignReviewReceipt({ featureId: "f1", previous: null, sources: sources(specV1), reviewer, report: pairReport("specs/f1/report-a.md", PUBLISHED, sha("another original")),
+    openFindingIds: ["F-FORMAT"], reviewedAt: "2026-10-10T00:00:00.000Z" });
+  assert.notEqual(other.sha256, first.sha256);
+});

+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// PF-16 (observed 2026-10-18, real greenfield project): the write-target extraction of `sed -i` named the sed SCRIPT
+// (or the value of -e / -f) as the written file, so GUARD-DEVPLAN-SHELL reported `File: s/^...` for a command whose real
+// target was under specs/. GNU sed: the first non-option operand is the script unless -e/-f/--expression/--file gives
+// one, then every non-option operand is a file. `-i[SUFFIX]` / `--in-place[=SUFFIX]` take a glued suffix only.
+// Each case lists the EXACT write-command candidates; nothing but the real file operand(s) may appear.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+const writeCommandCandidates = command => targetsOf(command).filter(t => t.lane === 'write-command').map(t => t.candidate);
+const F = 'specs/f/x.md';
+const sedShapes = [
+  [`sed -i 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i '1d' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i -n '1p' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i -E 's/(a)/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -Ei 's/(a)/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -ri 's/(a)/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -s -i 's/a/b/' a.md b.md`, ['a.md', 'b.md']],
+  [`sed -i -- 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -ni 's/a/b/p' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i.bak 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -ie 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]], // GNU: suffix `e`, the script is still the first operand
+  [`sed --in-place 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed --in-place=.bak 's/a/b/' ${F} specs/f/y.md`, [F, 'specs/f/y.md']],
+  [`sed --in=.bak 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  // script by option: every operand is a file (the PF-16 shape is the first of these)
+  [`sed -i -e 's/^a/b/' -e 's/c/d/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i -e 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i -es/a/b/ ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -ni -e 'p' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i --expression='s/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i --expression 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i --expr='s/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i -f fix.sed ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i --file=fix.sed ${F} specs/f/y.md`, [F, 'specs/f/y.md']],
+  [`sed -i --file fix.sed ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -nf fix.sed -i ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i ${F} -e 's/a/b/'`, [F]], // options may follow operands
+  [`sed -l 70 -i 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`sed -i --line-length=70 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  // the stash reader in the same command line contributes no candidate at all
+  [`git stash list | sed -i 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  [`git stash show | sed -i -e 's/a/b/' ${F}`, [F]],
+  // sed `w` command and `s///w` flag write a second file with -i: a real target, not mis-attributed
+  [`sed -i 'w out/side.txt' ${F}`, [F, 'out/side.txt']],
+  [`sed -i 's/a/b/w out/flag.txt' ${F}`, [F, 'out/flag.txt']],
+  // sed without an in-place option never was a writer here
+  [`sed -n 's/a/b/p' ${F}`, []],
+  [`sed -e 's/a/b/' ${F}`, []],
+];
+for (const [command, expected] of sedShapes) {
+  test('PF-16 sed -i: the write targets of `' + command + '` are exactly the file operands', () => {
+    assert.deepEqual(writeCommandCandidates(command), expected);
+  });
+}
+test('PF-16 sed -i: a detached empty suffix (BSD) or empty script (GNU) keeps both readings as candidates', () => {
+  const candidates = writeCommandCandidates(`sed -i '' 's/a/b/' ${F}`);
+  assert.ok(candidates.includes(F), 'the real file stays a candidate');
+  assert.ok(candidates.every(c => c === F || c === 's/a/b/'));

+  writeFileSync(join(f.root, `specs/${f.featureId}/review/design-review-1.original.md`), 'original report\n');
+  check('design-review receipt (sanitized report pair)', walkAction(DESIGN_REVIEW_RECEIPT_SCRIPT, 'create', '--repo', f.root, '--feature', f.featureId, '--report', reportPath,
+    '--original-report', `specs/${f.featureId}/review/design-review-1.original.md`, '--prd', f.sources.prd.path, '--spec', f.sources.spec.path, '--out', `specs/${f.featureId}/review/design-review-receipt-2.json`));
+  check('design-review receipt (delta)', walkAction(DESIGN_REVIEW_RECEIPT_SCRIPT, 'create', '--repo', f.root, '--feature', f.featureId, '--report', reportPath,
+    '--prd', f.sources.prd.path, '--spec', f.sources.spec.path, '--previous', receiptPath, '--open-finding', 'F-1', '--out', `specs/${f.featureId}/review/design-review-receipt-3.json`));
+  // 4. present-plan, then the approval transport in the spelling inspect renders after a presentation, then the phase switch
+  check('present-plan', stateCmd('present-plan', '--by', 'Fixture', '--review-receipt', receiptPath));
+  mkdirSync(join(f.root, 'scratch'), { recursive: true });
+  const requestPath = `scratch/design-approval-request-${walkDigest('request')}.json`;
+  writeFileSync(join(f.root, requestPath), '{}\n');
+  check('approve-plan (signature transport)', stateCmd('approve-plan', '--design-approval-request', requestPath));
+  check('approve-plan (attributed transport)', stateCmd('approve-plan', '--by', 'Fixture PO', '--design-approval-request', requestPath));
+  check('set-phase implementation', stateCmd('set-phase', '--phase', 'implementation'));
+  // 5. feature-package registration, the last step before the first implementation dispatch
+  check('feature-package registration', walkAction(REGISTER_FEATURE_PACKAGE_SCRIPT, '--root', f.root));
+  return { steps, out };
+}

+test('D64 integration: from a fresh repository to the first implementation dispatch every prescribed command is admitted by the real guards with zero overrides', async () => {
+  const hooks = await import(new URL('../../hooks/design-course-command-admission.test.mjs', import.meta.url));
+  const f = hooks.fixture('feature');
+  try {
+    const { steps, out } = await walkSteps(f, hooks);
+    const refusedSteps = steps.filter(step => step.code !== 0 || step.lane !== null);
+    assert.deepEqual(refusedSteps.map(step => `${step.label}: guard exit ${step.code}${step.lane ? `, dev-plan lane ${step.lane}` : ''}; command: ${step.rendered}; ${String(step.stderr).split('\n')[0]}`), [],
+      'a prescribed command is still refused by a real guard: report each with the file that must change');
+    assert.deepEqual(out.filter(entry => !entry.ok).map(entry => `${entry.label}: ${entry.detail}`), []);
+    assert.equal(steps.length >= 13, true, 'the walk covers every prescribed step');
+  } finally { f.cleanup(); }
+});

+test('D64 integration (implementation boundary): the next action inspect renders after approval is admitted by the real guards as spelled, absolute node executable included', async () => {
+  const { prepareApprovedItemCourseFixture } = await import(new URL('../test-item-approved-course-fixture.mjs', import.meta.url));
+  const approvedRoot = mkdtempSync(join(repo, 'scratch', 'alf-k11-approved-'));
+  const quiet = fn => { const before = console.log; console.log = () => {}; try { return fn(); } finally { console.log = before; } };
+  try {
+    quiet(() => prepareApprovedItemCourseFixture({ root: approvedRoot }));
+    const lines = [];
+    const before = console.log; console.log = (...v) => lines.push(v.join(' '));
+    try { assert.equal(run(['inspect'], { dir: approvedRoot, architectureEntryReadiness: () => ({ status: 'ready' }) }), 0); } finally { console.log = before; }
+    const inspected = JSON.parse(lines.join('\n'));
+    assert.equal(inspected.status, 'implementing');
+    const next = inspected.nextAction;
+    assert.equal(next.kind, 'command', JSON.stringify(next));
+    assert.equal(next.executable, process.execPath, 'the rendered executable is the absolute node path');
+    const action = { executable: next.executable, argv: next.argv };
+    // The fixture project is not governance-enforced, so the guard hooks would not run at all there; the closed-argv verdict is what an enforced,
+    // not-ready project applies to the same rendered command, and that is what must admit it.
+    assert.equal(isSanctionedLifecycleCommand(walkRender(action), approvedRoot), true, `the closed-argv catalogue refuses the command the Pipeline itself renders after approval: ${walkRender(action)}`);
+    assert.equal(devPlanShellRefusalHit(walkRender(action), approvedRoot, deps), null, `${walkRender(action)} is refused by the dev-plan shell lane after approval`);
+  } finally { rmSync(approvedRoot, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
+});

 test("C5 genuinely installed runner acceptance (host-held)", { skip: "native-pending: owner Elephant/PO host, due before AC-212 PO acceptance, host = a machine with qualified installed codex/claude/antigravity runners; the real readInstalledResult/verifyInstalledQualification ports behind createModelFamilyAcceptanceHost (model-family-acceptance.mjs) and the real createModelFamilyRuntimeHost resolveCurrentInvocationIdentity are not available in this session" }, () => {});
+
+// ======================================================================================================
+// Extension ALF-K20 (PO decision D99, 2026-10-22): the LEGACY V3 bootstrap (family activation inactive) follows a
+// new VERSION inside an approved FAMILY automatically; a NEW family still needs the PO. Also pins the open defect
+// pipeline.model-role-bootstrap-ignores-version-bumps-within-a-family. The family and the version order are
+// DECLARED (signed policy entries carry familyId + version tuple; the host supplies the catalogue records of the
+// existing pipeline.model-family-discovery.v1 shape) and compared with compareModelFamilyVersions; no model id string
+// is ever parsed for a family or a version. Old-style policies (no declaration) keep today's behaviour byte for byte.
+// The model-role-session.mjs / model-role-host-session.mjs source is kernel-protected: these cases are green only
+// with the protected diff applied.
+// ======================================================================================================
+import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { admitModelRoleBootstrap, modelRoleBootstrapReadback, resolveModelRoleBootstrap, resolveModelRoleSession,
+  validateModelRoleSessionReceipt } from "../model-role-session.mjs";
+import { admitModelRoleHostBootstrap, prepareModelRoleHostBootstrap } from "../model-role-host-session.mjs";
+import { createModelRoleHostStore } from "../model-role-host-store.mjs";
+import { verifyModelRoleApprovedPolicy } from "../model-role-approved-policy.mjs";
+import { functionalTaskRoutesForRunner, registeredFunctionalTaskRoutes } from "../model-role-route-source.mjs";
+
+const V3_EVIDENCE = "a".repeat(64);
+const V3_COMMIT = "b".repeat(40);
+const V3_AT = "2026-10-22T12:00:00.000Z";
+const V3_ROUTES = [{ runner: "claude", role: "frontier", effort: "high" }];
+// An approved policy entry; the family declaration is optional and always a (familyId, version) pair.
+const v3Entry = (modelId, rank, familyId, version, evidence = V3_EVIDENCE) => ({ modelId, rank, efforts: ["high"],
+  compatibilityEvidenceSha256: evidence, ...(familyId === undefined ? {} : { familyId, version }) });
+// One release record of a normalized discovery (pipeline.model-family-discovery.v1 records[] shape).
+const v3Release = (familyId, version, modelId, { record = {}, variant = {} } = {}) => ({
+  releaseId: `${familyId}-${version.join(".")}`, familyId, version, released: true, groupingEvidenceSha256: sha("grouping"),
+  variants: [{ modelId, canonicalModelId: null, aliasEvidenceSha256: null, visible: true, selectable: true,
+    efforts: ["high"], capabilityEvidenceSha256: sha("capability"), compatibilityBySlotSha256: {}, ...variant }],
+  ...record });
+const v3Route = ({ sessionId, approved, available, catalogue }) => ({ runner: "claude", role: "frontier", effort: "high",
+  sessionId, candidateCommit: V3_COMMIT, observedAt: V3_AT,
+  policy: { schema: "pipeline.model-role-policy.v1", runner: "claude", role: "frontier", approved },
+  availableModelIds: available, ...(catalogue === undefined ? {} : { familyCatalogue: catalogue }) });
+const v3Boot = (o, previousReceipts = []) => resolveModelRoleBootstrap({ sessionId: o.sessionId, configuredRoutes: V3_ROUTES,
+  routes: [v3Route(o)], previousReceipts });
+const v3Receipt = (o) => resolveModelRoleSession(v3Route(o)).receipt;
+const v3Readback = (current, previous) => modelRoleBootstrapReadback({ sessionId: current.sessionId, receipts: [current],
+  previousReceipts: [previous] });
+const OPUS_48 = v3Entry("claude-opus-4-8", 1, "opus", [4, 8]);
+const OPUS_CATALOGUE = [v3Release("opus", [4, 8], "claude-opus-4-8"), v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9"),
+  v3Release("opus", [4, 10], "claude-opus-4-10")];
+
+test("F1 an in-family bump is followed automatically: no review candidate, no acknowledgement, numeric order, catalogue order irrelevant", () => {
+  const first = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-old", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"],
+    catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0]] });
+  assert.equal(first.ok, true, first.code);
+  assert.equal(first.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-8");
+  assert.equal(first.receipts[0].familyId, "opus"); assert.deepEqual(first.receipts[0].version, [4, 8]);
+  for (const catalogue of [OPUS_CATALOGUE, [...OPUS_CATALOGUE].reverse()]) {
+    const next = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-new", approved: [OPUS_48],
+      available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9", "claude-opus-4-10"], catalogue }, first.receipts);
+    assert.equal(next.ok, true, next.code);
+    assert.equal(next.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-10", "4.10 beats 4.9 numerically, not lexicographically");
+    assert.deepEqual(next.reviewCandidates, [], "an in-family model is not a review candidate");
+    assert.equal(next.readback.mappings[0].change, "unchanged");
+    assert.equal(next.readback.mappings[0].previousModelId, "claude-opus-4-8");
+    assert.equal(next.readback.mappings[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-10");
+    assert.equal(next.readback.acknowledgementRequired, false);
+    assert.equal(validateModelRoleSessionReceipt(next.receipts[0]), true);
+    const admitted = admitModelRoleBootstrap({ sessionId: "s-new", configuredRoutes: V3_ROUTES, receipts: next.receipts,
+      previousReceipts: first.receipts });
+    assert.equal(admitted.ok, true, admitted.code); assert.equal(admitted.readbackSha256, next.readback.readbackSha256);
+  }
+});
+
+test("F2 a NEW family is never followed or grouped by name: it stays a review candidate and the receipt does not move", () => {
+  const first = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-old", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"], catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0]] });
+  const next = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-new", approved: [OPUS_48],
+    available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-sonnet-5", "claude-opus-5"],
+    // "claude-opus-5" LOOKS like Opus but the catalogue declares another family: it must not be grouped by its name.
+    catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], v3Release("sonnet", [5], "claude-sonnet-5"), v3Release("opus-next", [5], "claude-opus-5")] },
+  first.receipts);
+  assert.equal(next.ok, true, next.code);
+  assert.equal(next.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-8");
+  assert.deepEqual(next.reviewCandidates.map((c) => c.modelId), ["claude-opus-5", "claude-sonnet-5"]);
+  assert.equal(next.readback.mappings[0].change, "unchanged");
+  assert.equal(next.readback.acknowledgementRequired, false, "no receipt moved, so nothing to acknowledge; the PO sees the candidates");
+});
+
+test("F3 an unreleased, hidden, unselectable, effort-incapable, unrecognised or catalogue-less newer version changes nothing", () => {
+  const first = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-old", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"], catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0]] });
+  const never = (label, catalogueNewer, available = ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"], catalogue) => {
+    const next = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-new", approved: [OPUS_48], available,
+      catalogue: catalogue ?? [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], ...catalogueNewer] }, first.receipts);
+    assert.equal(next.ok, true, `${label}: ${next.code}`);
+    assert.equal(next.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-8", label);
+    assert.equal(next.readback.mappings[0].change, "unchanged", label);
+    assert.deepEqual(next.reviewCandidates.map((c) => c.modelId), ["claude-opus-4-9"], `${label}: still reported, never followed`);
+  };
+  never("unreleased", [v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9", { record: { released: false }, variant: { selectable: false } })]);
+  never("hidden", [v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9", { variant: { visible: false, selectable: false } })]);
+  never("unselectable", [v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9", { variant: { selectable: false } })]);
+  never("effort not supported", [v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9", { variant: { efforts: ["low"] } })]);
+  never("effort unknown", [v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9", { variant: { efforts: null, capabilityEvidenceSha256: null } })]);
+  never("not in the catalogue", []);
+  // catalogued but not offered by this host: nothing to follow and nothing to report
+  const absent = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-new", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"],
+    catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]] }, first.receipts);
+  assert.equal(absent.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-8"); assert.deepEqual(absent.reviewCandidates, []);
+  // no catalogue at all: today's behaviour, the name alone proves nothing
+  const bare = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-new", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"] }, first.receipts);
+  assert.equal(bare.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-8");
+  assert.deepEqual(bare.reviewCandidates.map((c) => c.modelId), ["claude-opus-4-9"]);
+});
+
+test("F4 negative controls: a different family, a downgrade, a one-sided declaration or a changed policy still require acknowledgement", () => {
+  // same policy, rank decides: sonnet (rank 2) wins when it is available, opus (rank 1) otherwise -> family changes.
+  const crossFamily = [OPUS_48, v3Entry("claude-sonnet-5", 2, "sonnet", [5])];
+  const opus = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p1", approved: crossFamily, available: ["claude-opus-4-8"] });
+  const sonnet = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p2", approved: crossFamily, available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-sonnet-5"] });
+  assert.equal(opus.policySha256, sonnet.policySha256);
+  const family = v3Readback(sonnet, opus);
+  assert.equal(family.mappings[0].change, "changed"); assert.equal(family.acknowledgementRequired, true);
+  // same family, both versions signed, the lower one is the survivor now -> downgrade
+  const both = [OPUS_48, v3Entry("claude-opus-4-9", 2, "opus", [4, 9])];
+  const high = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p1", approved: both, available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"] });
+  const low = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p2", approved: both, available: ["claude-opus-4-8"] });
+  assert.equal(high.policySha256, low.policySha256);
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(low, high).mappings[0].change, "changed");
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(low, high).acknowledgementRequired, true);
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(high, low).mappings[0].change, "unchanged", "the same two receipts in the upgrade direction need no acknowledgement");
+  // one entry declares the pair, the other does not
+  const oneSided = [OPUS_48, v3Entry("claude-opus-4-9", 2)];
+  const declared = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p1", approved: oneSided, available: ["claude-opus-4-8"] });
+  const bare = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p2", approved: oneSided, available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"] });
+  assert.equal(declared.policySha256, bare.policySha256);
+  assert.equal(Object.hasOwn(bare, "familyId"), false);
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(bare, declared).mappings[0].change, "changed");
+  // same family, higher version, but the signed policy itself changed -> the existing policy-change contract holds
+  const previous = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p1", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"], catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0]] });
+  const revised = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p2", approved: [v3Entry("claude-opus-4-8", 1, "opus", [4, 8], "c".repeat(64))],
+    available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"], catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]] });
+  assert.equal(revised.modelId, "claude-opus-4-9");
+  assert.notEqual(revised.policySha256, previous.policySha256);
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(revised, previous).mappings[0].change, "changed");
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(revised, previous).acknowledgementRequired, true);
+  // a previous old-style receipt against a new-style policy is one acknowledged adoption step
+  const oldStyle = v3Receipt({ sessionId: "p0", approved: [v3Entry("claude-opus-4-8", 1)], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"] });
+  assert.equal(v3Readback(previous, oldStyle).acknowledgementRequired, true);
+});
+
+test("F5 an old-style policy behaves exactly as before: same receipt keys, same receipt digest, a catalogue changes nothing", () => {
+  const route = v3Route({ sessionId: "golden-session", available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"],
+    approved: [v3Entry("claude-opus-4-8", 1), v3Entry("claude-opus-4-9", 2)] });
+  const plain = resolveModelRoleSession(route);
+  // golden values measured on the unchanged code at base ffdd0e812
+  assert.equal(plain.receipt.receiptSha256, "07d3ab0b433f715eb7fc2043b46cec543dd267baf237797a3f2df153b07140ce");
+  assert.equal(plain.receipt.policySha256, "59dc1f6a229e08dcbf859e85480dd07714b2899c7edba3c5d27ef393cd873291");
+  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(plain.receipt).sort(), ["candidateAtBootstrap", "compatibilityEvidenceSha256", "effort",
+    "modelId", "observedAt", "policySha256", "receiptSha256", "role", "runner", "schema", "sessionId"]);
+  const withCatalogue = resolveModelRoleSession({ ...route, familyCatalogue: [v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9"),
+    v3Release("opus", [4, 11], "claude-opus-4-11")], availableModelIds: [...route.availableModelIds, "claude-opus-4-11"] });
+  assert.equal(withCatalogue.receipt.receiptSha256, plain.receipt.receiptSha256, "no declaration -> the catalogue is inert");
+  assert.deepEqual(withCatalogue.unapprovedAvailableModelIds, ["claude-opus-4-11"]);
+  assert.equal(Object.hasOwn(withCatalogue, "familyFollow"), false);
+});
+
+test("F6 the policy declaration is a strict optional pair: malformed, one-sided or duplicate declarations are refused", () => {
+  const base = { sessionId: "s", available: ["claude-opus-4-8"] };
+  const ok = resolveModelRoleSession(v3Route({ ...base, approved: [OPUS_48] }));
+  assert.equal(ok.ok, true, ok.code);
+  assert.equal(validateModelRoleSessionReceipt(ok.receipt), true);
+  const refused = (entry) => resolveModelRoleSession(v3Route({ ...base, approved: [entry] })).code;
+  const { familyId, version, ...bare } = OPUS_48;
+  for (const bad of [
+    { ...bare, familyId: "opus" }, { ...bare, version: [4, 8] },
+    { ...OPUS_48, version: [] }, { ...OPUS_48, version: [4, -1] }, { ...OPUS_48, version: [4, 1.5] },
+    { ...OPUS_48, version: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9] }, { ...OPUS_48, version: "4.8" }, { ...OPUS_48, version: [4, "8"] },
+    { ...OPUS_48, familyId: "Opus" }, { ...OPUS_48, familyId: "" }, { ...OPUS_48, familyId: "o".repeat(33) },
+    { ...OPUS_48, familyId: 4 }, { ...OPUS_48, extra: true },
+  ]) assert.equal(refused(bad), "MODEL-ROLE-INPUT-INVALID", JSON.stringify(bad));
+  // two entries of one family may not claim the same version (zero padding is the same version)
+  const twin = resolveModelRoleSession(v3Route({ ...base, available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-8b"],
+    approved: [OPUS_48, v3Entry("claude-opus-4-8b", 2, "opus", [4, 8, 0])] }));
+  assert.equal(twin.code, "MODEL-ROLE-INPUT-INVALID");
+  // a forged receipt pair does not validate
+  assert.equal(validateModelRoleSessionReceipt({ ...ok.receipt, version: [9] }), false);
+  const { receiptSha256, familyId: dropped, ...rest } = ok.receipt;
+  assert.equal(validateModelRoleSessionReceipt({ ...rest, receiptSha256 }), false, "a half pair is not a receipt");
+});
+
+test("F7 a malformed catalogue or one that contradicts the signed declaration is refused concretely, never guessed around", () => {
+  const route = (catalogue) => v3Route({ sessionId: "s", approved: [OPUS_48],
+    available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"], catalogue });
+  const code = (catalogue) => resolveModelRoleSession(route(catalogue)).code;
+  assert.equal(code([OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]]), "MODEL-ROLE-SESSION-SELECTED");
+  for (const bad of [
+    "opus", {}, [{}], [{ ...OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], extra: 1 }], [{ ...OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], version: [] }],
+    [{ ...OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], released: "yes" }], [{ ...OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], variants: [] }],
+    [{ ...OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], familyId: "Opus" }],
+    [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], OPUS_CATALOGUE[0]],                                         // duplicate release
+    [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-8")],              // duplicate model id
+    [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], v3Release("opus", [4, 9], "claude-opus-4-9"),
+      v3Release("opus", [4, 9, 0], "claude-opus-4-9b", { record: { releaseId: "other" } })], // ambiguous version
+  ]) assert.equal(code(bad), "MODEL-ROLE-FAMILY-CATALOGUE-INVALID", JSON.stringify(bad).slice(0, 80));
+  // the catalogue places the approved model in another family or at another version than the PO declared
+  assert.equal(code([v3Release("sonnet", [4, 8], "claude-opus-4-8"), OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]]), "MODEL-ROLE-FAMILY-DECLARATION-MISMATCH");
+  assert.equal(code([v3Release("opus", [4, 7], "claude-opus-4-8"), OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]]), "MODEL-ROLE-FAMILY-DECLARATION-MISMATCH");
+  // the approved model is simply not in the catalogue: nothing to anchor on, nothing followed
+  const unanchored = resolveModelRoleSession(route([OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]]));
+  assert.equal(unanchored.receipt.modelId, "claude-opus-4-8");
+  assert.deepEqual(unanchored.unapprovedAvailableModelIds, ["claude-opus-4-9"]);
+});
+
+test("F8 a rollback to an older in-family version is a change that needs acknowledgement", () => {
+  const followed = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-1", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8", "claude-opus-4-9"],
+    catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0], OPUS_CATALOGUE[1]] });
+  assert.equal(followed.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-9");
+  const rolledBack = v3Boot({ sessionId: "s-2", approved: [OPUS_48], available: ["claude-opus-4-8"],
+    catalogue: [OPUS_CATALOGUE[0]] }, followed.receipts);
+  assert.equal(rolledBack.receipts[0].modelId, "claude-opus-4-8");
+  assert.equal(rolledBack.readback.mappings[0].change, "changed");
+  assert.equal(rolledBack.readback.acknowledgementRequired, true);
+});

+test("F9 a signed policy carrying the declaration verifies, an old-style signed policy still verifies, and tampering with the pair is refused", () => {
+  const rows = (selector) => [{ taskRoute: "duty.read", runner: "claude", role: "frontier", effort: "high", selector }];
+  const routeSource = source(rows("claude-opus-4-8"));
+  const sign = (approvedPolicies) => {
+    const approvalIntent = createModelRolePolicyApprovalIntent({ routeSource, approvedPolicies, candidateAtApproval: candidate });
+    return { schema: "pipeline.model-role-approved-policy.v1", candidateAtApproval: candidate, approvedPolicies, approvalIntent,
+      proof: proofFor(approvalIntent.sha256) };
+  };
+  const policyOf = (approved) => [{ runner: "claude", role: "frontier", effort: "high",
+    policy: { schema: "pipeline.model-role-policy.v1", runner: "claude", role: "frontier", approved } }];
+  const verify = (bundle) => verifyModelRoleApprovedPolicy({ bundle, routeSource, trustAnchors });
+  const declared = sign(policyOf([OPUS_48]));
+  assert.equal(verify(declared).ok, true, verify(declared).code);
+  assert.equal(verify(sign(policyOf([v3Entry("claude-opus-4-8", 1)]))).ok, true, "old-style signed policy: unchanged behaviour");
+  const tampered = structuredClone(declared); tampered.approvedPolicies[0].policy.approved[0].version = [4, 99];
+  assert.equal(verify(tampered).ok, false); assert.equal(verify(tampered).code, "MODEL-ROLE-POLICY-INTENT");
+  const stripped = structuredClone(declared); delete stripped.approvedPolicies[0].policy.approved[0].familyId;
+  delete stripped.approvedPolicies[0].policy.approved[0].version;
+  assert.equal(verify(stripped).ok, false, "removing the declaration after signing breaks the proof");
+  const swapped = structuredClone(declared); swapped.approvedPolicies[0].policy.approved[0].familyId = "sonnet";
+  assert.equal(verify(swapped).ok, false);
+});

+test("F10 the host bootstrap carries the observed catalogue to the resolver and a real private store admits the followed receipt", () => {
+  const sourceCodex = functionalTaskRoutesForRunner(registeredFunctionalTaskRoutes(), "codex");
+  const routeEntries = sourceCodex.configuredRoutes.map((route) => ({ ...route, modelId: `model-${route.runner}-${route.role}-${route.effort}`,
+    family: `${route.role}-${route.effort}` }));
+  const policyByRole = new Map();
+  for (const route of routeEntries) {
+    const key = `${route.runner}:${route.role}`;
+    if (!policyByRole.has(key)) policyByRole.set(key, { schema: "pipeline.model-role-policy.v1", runner: route.runner, role: route.role, approved: [] });
+    const list = policyByRole.get(key).approved;
+    list.push({ modelId: route.modelId, rank: list.length + 1, efforts: [route.effort], compatibilityEvidenceSha256: V3_EVIDENCE,
+      familyId: route.family, version: [1] });
+  }
+  const approvedPolicies = routeEntries.map(({ modelId, family, selector, ...route }) => ({ ...route, policy: policyByRole.get(`${route.runner}:${route.role}`) }));
+  const observationsFor = (withNext) => routeEntries.map(({ modelId, family, selector, ...route }) => ({ ...route, ok: true,
+    assurance: "installed-host-observed",
+    availableModelIds: withNext ? [modelId, `${modelId}-v2`] : [modelId],
+    familyCatalogue: [v3Release(family, [1], modelId, { variant: { efforts: [route.effort] } }),
+      ...(withNext ? [v3Release(family, [2], `${modelId}-v2`, { variant: { efforts: [route.effort] } })] : [])] }));
+  const input = (id, withNext, previousReceipts = []) => ({ sessionId: id, candidateCommit: "a".repeat(40), observedAt: "2026-10-22T00:00:00.000Z",
+    routeSource: sourceCodex, approvedPolicies, observations: observationsFor(withNext), previousReceipts });
+  const first = prepareModelRoleHostBootstrap(input("store-1", false));
+  assert.equal(first.ok, true, first.code);
+  const commonDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "model-role-k20-"));
+  try {
+    const verifyAuthority = () => ({ ok: true, approvedPolicies });
+    const store = createModelRoleHostStore(commonDir, { verifyAuthority });
+    const authority = { routeSource: sourceCodex };
+    const ack = { sessionId: "store-1", confirmed: true, readbackSha256: first.readback.readbackSha256 };
+    assert.equal(admitModelRoleHostBootstrap({ proposal: first, acknowledgement: ack, store, authority }).ok, true);
+    const next = prepareModelRoleHostBootstrap(input("store-2", true, first.receipts));
+    assert.equal(next.ok, true, next.code);
+    assert.ok(next.receipts.every((r) => r.modelId.endsWith("-v2")), "every route followed its family to version 2");
+    assert.deepEqual(next.reviewCandidates, []);
+    assert.equal(next.readback.acknowledgementRequired, false);
+    const admitted = admitModelRoleHostBootstrap({ proposal: next, acknowledgement: null, store, authority });
+    assert.equal(admitted.ok, true, admitted.code);
+    const held = store.read("store-2");
+    assert.equal(held.ok, true, held.code);
+    assert.ok(held.receipts.every((r) => typeof r.familyId === "string" && r.version.length === 1 && r.version[0] === 2),
+      "the stored receipts carry the followed family version");
+  } finally { rmSync(commonDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
+});
